{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_35","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import deque,defaultdict\nlines=Path('/app/dataset/genealogy_trees.txt').read_text().splitlines()\nH=len(lines); W=max(len(l) for l in lines)\ngrid=[list(l.ljust(W)) for l in lines]\nHSET=set('-~ ')\nVSET=set('|│')\n# relaxed box detection\nboxes=[]\nfor y in range(H):\n    row=''.join(grid[y])\n    pluses=[i for i,ch in enumerate(row) if ch=='+']\n    for a_idx,x0 in enumerate(pluses):\n        for x1 in pluses[a_idx+1:]:\n            if x1-x0 < 3 or x1-x0>40:\n                continue\n            if any(ch not in HSET for ch in row[x0+1:x1]):\n                continue\n            for y2 in range(y+2, min(H, y+10)):\n                if grid[y2][x0] \"'!= '\"'+' or grid[y2][x1] \"'!= '\"'+':\n                    continue\n                if any(ch not in HSET for ch in grid[y2][x0+1:x1]):\n                    continue\n                texts=[]\n                left_ok=right_ok=False\n                ok=True\n                for yy in range(y+1,y2):\n                    lch=grid[yy][x0]\n                    rch=grid[yy][x1]\n                    if lch in VSET: left_ok=True\n                    if rch in VSET: right_ok=True\n                    if lch not in VSET and rch not in VSET:\n                        ok=False; break\n                    inner=''.join(grid[yy][x0+1:x1])\n                    # allow letters/spaces only-ish; strip multiple spaces\n                    if '+' in inner:\n                        ok=False; break\n                    t=' '.join(inner.split())\n                    if t:\n                        texts.append(t)\n                if not ok or not texts or not (left_ok or right_ok):\n                    continue\n                name=' '.join(texts)\n                # filter names: mostly letters/spaces and length reasonable\n                if not any(c.isalpha() for c in name):\n                    continue\n                boxes.append({'name':name,'x0':x0,'x1':x1,'y0':y,'y1':y2})\n                break\n# dedupe overlapping identical coords with longer name maybe\nuniq={}\nfor b in boxes:\n    key=(b['x0'],b['x1'],b['y0'],b['y1'])\n    if key not in uniq or len(b['name'])>len(uniq[key]['name']):\n        uniq[key]=b\nboxes=list(uniq.values())\n# mask boxes\nconn_chars=set('+-~|/\\\\\\\\│')\nmask=[row[:] for row in grid]\nfor b in boxes:\n    for y in range(b['y0'],b['y1']+1):\n        for x in range(b['x0'],b['x1']+1):\n            mask[y][x]=' '\n# components on mask\ncomp=[[-1]*W for _ in range(H)]\ncomps=[]\nfor y in range(H):\n    for x in range(W):\n        if mask[y][x] in conn_chars and comp[y][x]==-1:\n            cid=len(comps)\n            q=deque([(y,x)])\n            comp[y][x]=cid\n            cells=[]\n            while q:\n                cy,cx=q.popleft()\n                cells.append((cy,cx))\n                for dy in (-1,0,1):\n                    for dx in (-1,0,1):\n                        if dy==0 and dx==0: continue\n                        ny,nx=cy+dy,cx+dx\n                        if 0<=ny<H and 0<=nx<W and comp[ny][nx]==-1 and mask[ny][nx] in conn_chars:\n                            comp[ny][nx]=cid\n                            q.append((ny,nx))\n            comps.append(cells)\n# find Dawid box\nb=next(bb for bb in boxes if bb['name']=='Dawid Majtyka')\nprint('Dawid',b)\n# top neighborhood comps\ncids=set()\nfor y in range(max(0,b['y0']-1), b['y0']+1):\n    for x in range(max(0,b['x0']-1), min(W,b['x1']+2)):\n        cid=comp[y][x]\n        if cid\"'!=-1: cids.add(cid)\nprint('\"'top cids',cids)\nfor cid in cids:\n    ys=[y for y,x in comps[cid]]; xs=[x for y,x in comps[cid]]\n    print('cid',cid,'bbox',min(xs),min(ys),max(xs),max(ys),'size',len(comps[cid]))\n    # nearby boxes touching this comp within 1 cell\n    touch=[]\n    cellset=set(comps[cid])\n    for bb in boxes:\n        touched=False\n        for y in range(bb['y0'],bb['y1']+1):\n            for x in range(bb['x0'],bb['x1']+1):\n                for dy in (-1,0,1):\n                    for dx in (-1,0,1):\n                        if (y+dy,x+dx) in cellset:\n                            touched=True; break\n                    if touched: break\n                if touched: break\n            if touched: break\n        if touched:\n            touch.append(bb['name'])\n    print('touch count',len(touch))\n    print(touch[:20])\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
